#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**The publicity device makes a conflict over preparation part of the material it seeks to capture.** The proposed appeal of an unguarded face depends on withholding notice, while both participants request preparation and Musubi explicitly extends the notice requirement to a manager. Player invokes the format itself against that request. The text therefore gives competing accounts of what responsible access would mean. Musubi continuing to speak about work and offering food does not remove the repeated filming objection, especially when Player subsequently reviews footage.

Alternative and limit: The sequence can function as comic backstage intimacy, and Player apology need not be cynical. Neither observation supplies a completed agreement, a legal judgment, every workplace practice or a claim that every member will respond the same way. Makeup is a condition Musubi names for presentation, not evidence that prepared public appearance is false.

**A compliment changes the exchange without resolving the question Musubi raised.** Player moves from the close-shot objection to his assessment of her beauty. Musubi written hesitation and stammering, and the distinct blushing/tense face resources, make the exchange affectively unsettled. Her exit and renewed no-filming request survive that shift. Player's internal uncertainty is also preserved, rather than becoming privileged access to her feelings.

Alternative and limit: Embarrassment, irritation, affection and surprise can coexist; the exact mix is not uniquely diagnosed. A genre shorthand or an assumption of secret consent would flatten the explicit objection. No reciprocal romance, sustained anger or hidden complete thought is established.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**The interview takes shape through the participants ordinary tempo and associations.** Repeated drinking/sigh text precedes Player request, and his permission to keep drinking meets a reluctance expressed as not knowing what to say. Shizuka reassurance turns the manager into a food metaphor, which Shinju redirects toward shop supply and Shizuka toward her grandfather. A presumed promotional topic becomes an exchange organized by their familiar concerns before Player reasserts the agenda.

Alternative and limit: The conversation can be friendly distraction as well as an obstacle to the interview. No completed purchase, verified fresh stock, independently observed grandfather reaction or deliberate coordinated sabotage is shown. Comparing this opening withChapter1does not prove that Player consciously learned a lesson between them.

**Shizuka's answer locates difficulty within the work even when Player treats it as a digression.** She explicitly places the unwanted association during singing and describes suppressing laughter as difficult. The lyric word becomes sound material for a pun, making verbal attention both an artistic task and a competing comic process. Player wish for a singing-related answer reveals his narrower expected account rather than independently demonstrating that she gave no work-related information.

Alternative and limit: Her account is self-report, not an inspected recording take, general concentration diagnosis or proof of deficient technique. The exact song and how the word is sung remain unidentified; written laughter does not reveal acoustic delivery.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0109.json__0496916636978e85?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`.

**Smiling becomes a contested work requirement and a desired relational sight.** Shinju challenges a direction attributed to Coney; Shizuka's answers with affectionate appreciation of the smile and a report of peer efforts. A question and sensational frame are not confirmation; no boyfriend identity is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`.
- P26-30: Momoka prompts Kajika again. Kajika explicitly takes Hime as a model for advertising home business, promotes魚晴fish and filleting service, and uses a shop-call style. Sui supplies a tofu-miso-soup/grilled-fish meal association, directly asks fans to support both shops and says she visits daily. The service, quality and frequency are the speakers statements; no new sales audit or completed fan response is shown. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`.
- P31-33: In reporter style Momoka summarizes girls eating, sleeping and singing as growing into aspiring idols, temporarily returns what she calls the camera and signs off from the scene. Her initial request named a microphone, while the ending names a camera; both textual object references are retained without inventing the exact unseen handover. Player initially echoes thanks in that style, then objects that nobody discussed the new song. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`.
- P34-37: Kajika values their energetic appeal and hopes for customers. Hime reassures her and imagines a rush of customers tomorrow. Player says distribution is still far ahead and again asks for new-song talk. The ending therefore preserves a mismatch between their immediate optimism and the actual publication timetable as Player states it, not an achieved customer surge. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**Rest, hunger and family skill remain part of the work setting rather than evidence that the idols lack commitment.** The break is explicitly after singing, and Kajika connects exhaustion with how much effort the new song draws. Food wishes differ: Sui imagines a future meal, Momoka wants somebody else to cook meat, and Kajika answers through concrete tofu/fish skills shared with Hime. The bodily and household concerns coexist with recording labor; the later comic summary gathers them without measuring a developmental rule.

Alternative and limit: No meal is actually made, no nutritional/medical claim is established and no universal work ethic follows from one break. Momoka request does not prove permanent laziness, and others cooking capacities do not certify an adequate labor distribution.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=1#/Pages/1`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=2#/Pages/2`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=3#/Pages/3`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=4#/Pages/4`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`.

**The interviewer role becomes portable, carrying its pressure as well as its creative opportunities.** Momoka is first the subject, then borrows the apparatus, selects the next subject, asks for sudden self-praise, supplies a sensational frame and closes with a field-reporter formula. Kajika and Hime initially struggle with that demand. The change redistributes control over the promotional narrative without making every peer-led prompt automatically less intrusive than Player ambush format.

Alternative and limit: This is temporary play and a reported apparatus handover, not a permanent journalism job, a new manager identity or a formal delegation policy. Exact physical camera/microphone handling is not rendered; Player surprise does not expose a complete private motive or amount to opposition to every improvisation.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=10#/Pages/10`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=11#/Pages/11`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`.

**Family-shop promotion offers a situated answer to the request for personal appeal.** Hime reaches a practiced family-business identity when an abstract self-description stalls. Kajika then names Hime example and adapts it to her own home shop. The existingUNIT0005and0006records make these ongoing work/care attachments legible, while Kajika apron visibly carries魚晴. Publicity can express those attachments as well as promote an idol product.

Alternative and limit: The existing records preserve unequal/unresolved staffing, care costs, ownership and financial questions. This local choice neither traps either character wholly in family duty nor proves a fully independent long-term idol program. Claims of cheapness, quality and frequent patronage remain interested speech; no new customers, wages or measured profit follows.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=12#/Pages/12`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`.

**The love-to-scandal sequence stages the construction and correction of a claim.** Sui begins with care/love invested in tofu; Momoka shifts to passionate romance and reporter scandal, Kajika translates that into a boyfriend question, and Sui joins the question prompted by her own original wording. Hime explicitly rejects the reading and identifies the lexical starting point. Distinct utterance statuses and receivers matter: the scene does not merely announce a romance and move on.

Alternative and limit: Playful teasing, affectionate endorsement and embarrassment can coexist. The denial addresses the current allegation and is not a complete history of Hime relationships or orientation. Kajika gendered boyfriend wording belongs to her question; it creates no unseen partner, no new identity route and no proof that Sui knowingly lies.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=20#/Pages/20`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=21#/Pages/21`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=22#/Pages/22`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`.

**Kajika improvisation and Sui endorsement turn familiar labor into a shared public-facing resource.** Kajika first seeks a substitute, then recognizes a usable procedure and delivers her own shop call, including a concrete service. Sui joins the two businesses through ordinary meal associations and addresses the prospective fans directly. Hime answers Kajika hopes with confident support. Those actor-specific initiatives are more informative than a uniform description that everybody simply cooperates.

Alternative and limit: The scene does not prove that all food claims are objectively correct, that every family member approved the promotion, or that shop and idol labor are sustainably combined. Prior independent promotion/illness stories retain their own intervals and cannot supply this scene commercial outcome.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=13#/Pages/13`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`.

**Producing a lively recording and reaching an audience are different temporal achievements.** Momoka signs off, Kajika values the energy of the appeal and Hime anticipates a tomorrow rush. Player finally supplies the delayed-distribution constraint. His frustration with absent song information coexists with the fact that the subjects have offered candid interests and family attachments, part of the broader wish he named atP9. The recording can thus exceed one agenda without already becoming a successful release.

Alternative and limit: No exact release date, final edit, actual audience, store demand or completed failure is shown. Momoka growth formula is her performed summary, not a universal psychology/narrator doctrine. The correction is local knowledge, not retrospective proof that all earlier aspirations were insincere.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=9#/Pages/9`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=31#/Pages/31`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=33#/Pages/33`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=34#/Pages/34`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0110.json__396ec1f03bb21ed3?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`.

**Visual regrouping distributes attention within one ordinary office and four continuing identities.** The same-background crossfades and slot shifts focus changing pairs/trios and finally a four-person arrangement, while their distinct clothing and facial variants remain bound to the same source-labelled actors. Momoka starry reporter expression, Hime grin/fluster/protest, Kajika surprised-to-shop-call smile and Sui supportive-to-questioning shift make the information chain visibly differentiated. Kajika fish-shop apron also gives ordinary work an enduring visual presence.

Alternative and limit: No character split follows from costume/resource variants, and slot clearing need not mean literal departure. No source-bound prop image shows the microphone/camera or a boyfriend; encoded shake/BGM004to006/fades are not heard or observed runtime performance.

#### Static form and original exit horizon

- All22current occurring reconstructed source-bound sprite stills actually viewed on one complete six-row contact, including the last two Kajika variants. Hime has short blond hair, white/teal sailor uniform and hands at hips; Sui short blue hair, white/navy sailor uniform with yellow tie and hands at hips. Their literal speaker labels and distinct resources remain two existing people, not a machine-ID or gender inference from the shared type of pose.
- Momoka keeps a green side ponytail and a highly decorated pink jacket over colorful clothing/plaid skirt. **A shared publicity frame competes with different desired forms of personal address.** Susu treats interview access as an opportunity to address Darling; Sumire initially insists on a two-person interview and distinguishes that from appealing to the manager. Player later asks for a personally important recipient, yet redirects both answers toward a public-facing frame. Being seen, addressing someone and being fit for a fan audience are related but different demands.

Alternative and limit: Sumire objection can include image participation, interpersonal discomfort and other feelings together; it need not be reduced to secretly wanting the same thing as Susu. Susu interpretation of Player embarrassment is not direct access to his intention. No universal audience preference or company rule is established.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**The family closure changes the criterion for a usable interview without erasing the earlier scenes.** Player explicit four-part recap makes these five chapters one recording-interview sequence. His initial criterion treats digression as failure. Sawara accepts the variety as evidence of the group relation the song is meant to convey, and Player later accepts Haru interview under that revised account. The closure offers a new reading of what the footage can communicate rather than demonstrating a technically completed promotional product.

Alternative and limit: The revision belongs to these speakers at this endpoint. It does not make Musubi filming objections, Shinju face privacy, the earlier gossip question or Sumire disputed addressee disappear. No retrospective blanket consent, adopted publicity rule, final edit or proven reception follows.

### Whole-family synthesis and separate closure

**An interview becomes a contested relation of access.** Player approaches Musubi and Rona with a candid recording scheme attributed to Coney. Musubi objects to the unannounced filming and closeness. Praise of her donut contribution and recording ability can produce cooperation with conversation while renewed filming objections remain. Rona offers coffee, a spill interrupts, and Player reports protecting the camera while her concern and apology continue.

The family begins by showing that productive group participation and willingness to have every moment recorded are different relations. Musubi can be generous toward others and competent in recording while disputing camera access. Rona care is enacted through an attempted helpful service that has a contrary immediate result. These particular tensions give the later defense of ordinary variety something more demanding than an uncomplicated blooper montage to reinterpret.

Alternative and limit: The camera survival is Player report. No final recording, blanket consent, intent to damage equipment, stable incompetence or global trust rule is established. Retain the source-literal name ビバド; the frozen private shorthand Romanization was unverified.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**Camera confidence and suitability for this particular production are different competencies.** Ume belittles Hina nervousness and expects approval after her own camera-ready performance. The director then asks for a different acting quality, creating a local reversal: confidence in a familiar public manner does not guarantee a match to the requested song image. Hina apprehension and Ume miscalibration can coexist without one becoming proof that the other is the better musician or person.

Alternative and limit: The director gives a situated production judgment, not an omniscient verdict that Ume is essentially fake or untalented. Neither the performance nor the song has been heard or watched as a PV. The image criterion remains underspecified at this endpoint.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**A professionally approved smile does not transparently disclose the meaning behind it.** The director approves the flower-gazing image and Hina admires its polish. When she asks how to reproduce it, Emoco pairs an ordinary instruction to think of enjoyable things with conspicuously misanthropic examples. The contrast distinguishes an effective public expression from a single guaranteed agreeable inner content. Hina lack of useful instruction matters: technical-looking ease is not automatically transferable as care for another person.

Alternative and limit: Emoco examples may be dark teasing rather than a literal account of what she thought during the take; the source does not settle that. No hidden murderous intention, ecological truth, universal falseness of smiling or acoustic acting quality is inferred.

### Whole-family synthesis and separate closure

**A production brief unsettles the familiar hierarchy of camera confidence.** The unnamed director announces4U PV/jacket rehearsal and a short timetable. Hina states camera anxiety. Ume dismisses it, Emoco attacks her habitual persona and Hina tries to moderate the exchange. Ume then performs cheerful lines, expects approval and receives a request to act more naturally in line with the song image.

The first reversal separates confidence from suitability for a particular work brief. The camera-ready manner visible to bandmates is not automatically the natural quality requested by the director. The scene thus introduces public-image labor as a negotiated practice rather than a direct expression of one settled inner self. Emoco reluctant greeting still recognizes work; Hina remains both vulnerable participant and intervening member.

**Approval of an image does not make its method useful to another member.** Emoco flower-gazing smile receives direct approval and Hina praise. Ume disputes it and Emoco answers with insults. The outwardly approved expression and the proposed inner method pull apart. Emoco examples may be teasing, and the source does not grant a transparent window on what she actually thought during the take. **A collective proposal takes effect through support, irritation and practical mediation.** Hina explicitly endorses handholding to the photographer. Ume predicts Emoco will mock it; Emoco actually calls the idea lovely while adding insults. Ume threatens to exclude her, Emoco makes a self-serving sales forecast and the photographer asks what to do. Hina gives the definite three-person arrangement and asks for both hands; Ume agrees. Hina promise not to let go is met by a terse reply and a mundane toilet objection, and she laughs. The director later reports the decision and costume change and calls the actual shoot.

The shared image is neither an effortless leader command nor the result of everyone adopting one sentimental voice. Hina gives the proposal operational form, while Emoco explicit approval and abrasion remain simultaneous. The ordinary reply to never letting go can temper a totalizing physical promise without negating the expressed attachment. A negotiated local image can be made amid continuing differences.

Alternative and limit: The predicted mockery is Ume own quotation, not an actual Emoco line. The sales forecast is not business data; the exclusionary retort is not completed expulsion. **The released image acquires a public reading without resolving the participant objection.** The coda puts the band on a street-like background, still arguing. Youth A asks whether the others bought the new record, Youth B explicitly confirms a purchase, and the three local youths give favourable image/jacket appraisals, with Youth C treating partly hidden faces as intentional style. Hina infers good reception, Emoco credits herself and praises Hina photo. Ume asks why her back-facing image was selected. Hina does not know; Emoco offers a possible unlike-Ume expression explanation, and Hina starts praising that expression before Ume stops her.

The production sequence reaches release and a bounded audience response, yet Ume desired visibility and protection of a particular expression remain unsettled. Selection and public interpretation do not simply reproduce the initiating member intention. The family can therefore end with a shared act and a successful local reception while retaining participant disagreement, unfamiliar expression and everyday abrasive friendship as unfinished matters.

Alternative and limit: The three youths share one unfeatured outline asset but are three separate text labels, with no cross-document identity inference. Their preferences do not establish total sales or universal approval. The earlier body/privacy/help tensions receive no explicit institutional repair.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**A promotional travel motif reveals an ordinary limit on Kajika opportunity to leave.** The costume conversation moves from an imagined journey to Kajika current account of shop and idol work. Her explanation of why family trips are rare and why even friend trips are difficult gives the travel motif a material context in everyday time. Her lack of a particular desired destination is equally part of the evidence, so the episode cannot simply be narrated as recovering an already confessed suppressed dream.

Alternative and limit: The shop daily opening is Kajika report, not a complete staffing/schedule audit. No wage, ownership, coercion diagnosis, total emotional state or exact duration since a trip is supplied. The new song title and actual performance remain unnamed/unheard here.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**The first stop tests whether travel can be an altered relation to a familiar place.** Shinju sets a demanding criterion: ordinary passage past the park seems incompatible with travel. The carved-name account and equipment detail then supply unknown aspects within that familiar setting, giving Sawara stated reinterpretation concrete local material. The chapter can offer this expanded meaning of travel even while the destination remains within the bounded city route established earlier.

Alternative and limit: The discovery does not erase the lack of time or replace the reported shop-work cover that made the outing possible. No claim that local outings meet every travel desire, that distance never matters or that Kajika original constraints are solved permanently is established.

#### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**The airport widens travel from a local experience to imagined work and possible separation.** Unlike the prior familiar stops, the airport lets Kajika encounter a visibly different environment and an institution associated with departures. Sawara moves among a reported Nanasta live, international recognition, future work and the excitement of meetings/beginnings/farewells. Kajika can share the place excitement while still finding overseas work difficult to imagine. The possible future has opened without becoming an achieved trip.

Alternative and limit: The reported live-before-joining relation retains its hearsay scope and no exact date. #### Interpretation, reasoning and alternatives

**Kajika's final evaluation supplies a concrete account of benefit that the earlier cases deliberately left open.** The return creates a moment in which the intended recipient can answer. Her enjoyment is specific: walking various places without a purpose was unusual for her. This gives the outing value in her own words, beyond Sawara assurance or Shinju critique. The earlier gratitude, surprise and doubts remain accurate states at their original horizons; her later answer is an additional result rather than permission to rewrite them as already resolved.

Alternative and limit: Kajika statement does not prove uniform pleasure in every imposed activity or waive her earlier startled responses at the school. Nor does Shinju different evaluation make Kajika account merely false politeness. The source does not provide complete private motives or a universal measure of successful care.

Alternative and limit: The final intention claim is Sawara own account, and Shinju certainty is her appraisal. No omniscient planning record is supplied. ### Whole-family synthesis and separate closure

**A costume journey meets the ordinary work that limits time away.** The new-song outfit design prompts talk about travel. Kajika reports daily shop opening, family-trip rarity, shop help and idol work, but says she has no particular destination in mind and has not worried much. Sawara announces a good idea to skeptical Shinju; later the flag and tour-conductor introductions offer Kajika a world tour bounded to7th. When she asks about shop help, Sawara says the manager is covering it, and departure is urged while Kajika remains surprised.

The imaginative trip begins through a material relation of time and work, not simply a preexisting confessed wish for a distant destination. A reported substitute makes the outing possible in Sawara account, while the recipient information gap and Shinju qualified participation keep initiative and assent distinct. The two visible objects make the theme concrete without changing the sisters into new people.

Alternative and limit: Player shop work is reported rather than independently witnessed. The displayed costume is an object, not worn; the flag does not establish a travel company or overseas departure. No new wage, ownership, staffing settlement, numeric age or full recipient evaluation is supplied at this opening.
